Log-sum-exp objective f_β(a)=logsumexp(β conv(a,a)) (with sum=1). Use ADAM on simplex:
- grad = 2β ∑_k p_k a_{k-j}, p_k softmax(β conv).
- We can compute pressure[j] = ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m] p[m+j] using FFT (as earlier). Let's verify with convolution q_j? For pressure[j] = ∑_k p_k a_{k-j}. Let m=k-j => k=m+j. M ranges 0..n-1, k 0..2n-2. So k-j valid iff 0<=k-j<n i.e., j<=k<=j+n-1. Thus pressure[j]=∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a_m p_{m+j}. This is cross-correlation of a and p. Using FFT: define a_rev = a[::-1], convolve a_rev with p: (a_rev * p)[j+n-1] = ∑_{m} a_rev[m] p[n-1+m - (j+n-1)?]. Let's recompute cleanly.

We want C[j] = ∑_{m=0}^{n-1} a[m] p[j+m], j=0..n-1.
Let r = n-1-m => a[m]=a_rev[n-1-r]? Or define a_flip[t]=a[n-1-t]. Convolution h = a_flip * p. h[s]=∑_{t=0}^{n-1} a_flip[t] p[s-t]. Set s=j+n-1:
h[j+n-1]=∑_t a[n-1-t] p[j+n-1-t]. Let m=n-1-t => t=n-1-m, j+n-1-t = j+m. sum_m a[m] p[j+m]. Yes. So C = h[n-1:2n-1] (length n) if h length up to 3n-2. Great.
FFT length Nfft >= len(a_flip)+len(p)-1 = (n-1)+(2n-1)-1=3n-2. But p length 2n-1. We can use Nfft next pow2 >=3n-2. That's ~3/2 of conv length? For n=4096, 12k, fine. Or use scipy.signal.fftconvolve; but implement manually.

- Optimization loop: Use adaptive logsumexp Adam.
  Pseudocode for optimize_simplex(n, init, time_limit):
    a = init normalized (sum=1, floor small >0)
    b = adam one, m=adam two
    beta = start e.g. 5 (or n? Need β*conv. Since conv ~1/n, β should scale ~n to make β*conv O(10). For n=128, 1/n=0.0078; β=1000 -> β*peak ~8. We can set target peak logit ~10: beta = 10/(maxconv) initially, adapt.
    For iter:
      conv = fft_conv(a)
      maxc = max(conv)
      objective = logsumexp(beta*conv)
      if maxc < best: update
      # softmax stable: z=beta*(conv-maxc); p=exp(z)/sum
      # grad = 2 * pressure (pressure via FFT)
      # normalize grad: maybe subtract mean to keep sum zero? Adam on simplex; after update project.
      # Adam:
      m = β1 m + (1-β1) grad
      v = β2 v + (1-β2) grad^2
      mhat=m/(1-β1^t); vhat=v/(1-β2^t)
      a = a - lr * mhat/sqrt(vhat+eps)
      a = clip(a, 0, inf)
      a = a / a.sum() # simpler than full simplex projection; if after clip sum>0. This maintains average but may lose some? It's like multiplicative? Using additve projection by normalization not true simplex; negative clipped mass not redistributed optimally but okay. Could use project_simplex for better.
      # Evaluate exact every few iterations.
      # Adapt beta: as maxc decreases, beta maybe increase to sharpen: beta = target / maxc, target slowly increase to 20 or 50.
      # Adapt lr: start maybe 0.5 / sqrt(n)? For normalized simplex with gradient magnitude? Need tune.

But additive Adam may push negative and clip heavily. Multiplicative update might better preserve nonnegativity:
   a = a * exp(-eta * grad_normalized); project simple by normalization.
Gradient of logsumexp: pressure positive; η small.
Multiplicative update tends to zero some entries, which may be good. Need choose η based on β and gradient scale. Could combine with occasional full simplex projection using warm start.

Top-level:
```
def softmax(x):
    x=x-np.max(x)
    e=np.exp(x)
    return e/e.sum()
```
For stability with large x, use max.

```
def conv_fft(a): ...
def logit_objective(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x); conv=conv_fft(a); z=beta*conv; maxz=z.max(); return maxz+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-maxz)))
def logit_jac(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x); conv=conv_fft(a); z=beta*conv; maxz=z.max(); w=np.exp(z-maxz); p=w/w.sum(); c=pressure(a,p); Ga=2*beta*? Wait pressure already sum p*a_{...}; ∂ logsumexp /∂a_j = beta * derivative q? q derivative 2 a_{k-j}; so Ga=2*beta * pressure. However p=exp(β q_k)/sum. If we use z=β conv, derivative of logsumexp wrt a_j = β * p^T ∂conv/∂a_j = β*2*pressure =2β pressure. Yes. But if objective = logsumexp(βconv), fine.
    S=dot(Ga,a)
    return a*(S-Ga)
```

Let's design an alternative iterative method "multiplicative annealing" that might be more global and fast:
```
def multiplicative_optimize(a, n, time_limit, start_beta, ns):
    a=a/a.sum(); maxc=conv.max(); beta=target/maxc (target grows)
    eta = 0.5/(beta? maybe)
    for it in range(max_iters):
       conv=conv_fft(a); maxc=...
       score=2*n*maxc
       if score<best: store
       z=beta*conv; p=softmax
       c=pressure(a,p)
       # normalize pressure to have mean 1? Since pressure scale ~? For uniform, pressure ~? Let's scale c = c / c.mean()
       # update a = a * exp(-eta * (c - 1)) maybe to keep sum approx
       a = a * np.exp(-eta*(c - c.mean()))
       a = np.clip(a,0,None); a/a.sum()
       # adapt eta: if score improved, keep; else reduce
       # target beta increase slowly: beta = target / maxc, target=min(target*1.05, 30)
```
This is similar to mirror descent, likely faster and preserves positivity. Could run for many iterations per start. It may converge to local minima. Use as warm-up before L-BFGS.

However, gradient of logsumexp for mirror descent with entropy regularizer: dimensionless objective logsumexp(βq)/β? Let's ensure step size. If we use a <- exp(-η ∂F/∂a)/normalization, ∂F/∂a=2β pressure. For large β, step huge. We can set pressure normalized to mean 1 and use η independent: a <- a*exp(-η*(press - meanpress)). This is like minimizing max with multiplicative weights, analogous to solving saddle point. Should be stable if η<1 maybe. Use η between 0.05 and 0.5.

The pressure c[j]=∑ p_k a_{k-j}. Since a sum1, p sum1, c sum =∑_j ∑_k p_k a_{k-j}=∑_k p_k * sum valid a_{k-j}. Not necessarily 1, but O(1). Normalize c to mean over j=1. Then update log a -= η(c-1). Entries at high pressure decrease. This should reduce peak contributions.

We can also use exact top peaks: set p_k = 1/top_count for k in top L by conv. Then pressure concentrates. Adaptive L increases as beta increases.

Let's think about convergence. Multiplicative update might drive some a_i to zero, increasing variance, but if score good maybe. We can prevent too small by floor e.g. a_max(a, 1e-12) before norm.

Could also use "simulated annealing" perturbations: occasionally multiply random block by exp noise, if exact score improves accept. This can escape local minima. The previous block perturb around constant failed, but around optimized asymmetric sequences may help.

Given time, a hybrid plan in final program:
1. For several n, generate starts.
2. Run a "fast_iter" multiplicative update for a limited number of iterations or until no improvement.
3. Run L-BFGS-B from best points for remaining time.
4. Run a final local exact coordinate search on best sequence.
5. Return best (list of floats) maybe scale sum to 1 (or any). Need ensure no NaN/inf.

Let's analyze logsumexp L-BFGS on softmax for n=32. Objective depends on β. We can run one long "annealed" optimization per start:
```
def run_lbfgs_start(x0, n, time_limit, beta_start_factor=5):
    a=softmax(x0); maxc=conv(a).max(); beta=beta_start_factor/maxc
    x=x0 - x0.mean()
    for stage in range(stages):
        res=optimize(logit_objective, x, jac=logit_jac, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': ..., 'ftol': ...})
        x=res.x
        a=softmax(x); maxc=conv(a).max(); update best
        beta *= 1.3
        x = x - x.mean()
    return best
```
But `scipy.optimize.minimize` maxiter time not directly; we can limit maxiter per stage small. For n=32, maxiter=1000 maybe okay; for n=256, maybe 200.

Mirror descent might be more robust for this nonconvex problem. Let's consider implementing a gradient-free but stable "exponential family" optimization:
At each iteration, choose weights p_k = softmax(β q_k). Update log a_i -= η * (∂/∂a_i logsumexp)/?? Actually if we use F = logsumexp(β q)/β, the Bregent divergence in log domain, natural gradient under sum constraint? The update a * exp(-η F_a) is exact mirror descent with entropy. We can tune η. This may be simpler and faster than L-BFGS. Let's derive step size.
For F_β = (1/β) logsumexp(β q), gradient wrt a = 2 p_k q? derivative: (1/β)*β*2 pressure=2 pressure. So independent of β! Interesting: if we use F_β = logsumexp/β, gradient =2 pressure. Mirror descent update with entropy: log a <- log a - η*2 pressure (and maybe subtract mean to preserve sum). So β only affects p (peak focus), not step size. We can use pressure normalized. This is nice. We want minimize max q, use annealed β. The update:
```
p = softmax(beta*conv)
c = pressure(a,p)
c = c / c.mean()
a = a * exp(-eta*(c - 1))
a = clip≥0; normalize
```
This is stable. As β increases, p focuses on active max, driving down contributions. It's like multiplicative weights update for bilinear constraints. It might find flat solutions.

We can use exact top peaks instead of softmax: For top L coefficients, p=1/L, c=average pressure over peaks. β annealing controls L effectively. We can implement both: soft p and then top-focal: take p, set p = top L by index? Or p from softmax with adaptive β.

This method can run many iterations (FFT O(n log n)). For n=256, each iteration: conv FFT len512, pressure FFT len ~768, very fast (<0.001s?). Could run 100k iterations in minutes, allowing extensive exploration. It may converge to local minima but with random restarts and annealing, good.

Let's implement SLSQP minimax:
Top-level functions:
```
def minmax_y_objective(y):
    return float(y[-1])

def minmax_eq_fun(y):
    return float(np.sum(y[:-1]) - 1.0)

def minmax_ieq_fun(y):
    a = y[:-1]
    t = y[-1]
    q = conv_fft(a)  # for n small, fft okay
    # due numerical negative? t - q maybe >0
    return t - q
```
Constraints: `cons=[{'type':'eq','fun':minmax_eq_fun},{'type':'ineq','fun':minmax_ieq_fun}]`. Bounds: [(0,1000)]*(n+1). Initial y = [a, t]. Need t > maxq. Set t = maxq*1.1+1e-6.

Let's implement L-BFGS start for n small/medium:
Top-level objective and jac have beta and lam? We'll keep simple no lam.
```
def logit_objective(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x)
    q=conv_fft(a)
    z=beta*q
    zmax=z.max()
    return zmax + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zmax)))

def logit_jacobian(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x)
    q=conv_fft(a)
    z=beta*q
    zmax=z.max()
    w=np.exp(z-zmax); p=w/w.sum()
    c=pressure(a,p)
    Ga=2*beta*c
    S=float(np.dot(Ga,a))
    return a*(S-Ga)
```
Potential overflow if beta*q large (e.g., beta=10000, q max0.01 ->100), exp(100) okay but stable subtract max. If beta even 1e6, zmax 10? Actually subtract, exp up to 0, min -large; fine. `zmax` computed before subtract; if q has tiny values, beta*q may be large but finite if beta <1e300. We set beta ≤1e6.

def lbfgs_anneal(x0, n, deadline, beta_start=5.0, stages=8, maxiter_stage=200):
    for stage in range(stages):
       if time>deadline: break
       beta = beta_start * (1.5**stage) / (1/n?) Wait beta target relative. If q~1/n, beta_start logit = beta*qmax. We want zmax around beta_start. So set beta = beta_start / maxq_current. Then zmax = beta_start. Do that.
       beta = beta_start / max(conv(softmax(x)).max(),1e-15)
       res=optimize(logit_objective, x, jac=logit_jacobian, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':maxiter_stage, 'ftol':1e-12})
       x=res.x - res.x.mean()
       update best
       if best improved little: beta_start*=1.5
    return best
```

  sigma=0.2

Since derivative of q_k wrt a_i = 2 a_{k-i}; for F=logsumexp(q)/qmax? gradient proportional to pressure. We'll use `a_i *= exp(-eta * (pressure_i / mean_pressure))`, normalize. This changes sum slightly; normalize. It reduces a_i where they participate (with weight p) in high-convolution positions.

Does this monotonically reduce max? Not guaranteed but should. We can adapt eta small (0.05-0.5). If max decreases, increase eta; if oscillates, decrease.

FFT implementation using `np.fft.rfft` and `irfft`:
```
def conv_fft(a):
    n=len(a)
    N=1 << (2*n-2).bit_length()
    fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
    c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N, norm=None)[:2*n-1] # irfft returns length N
    c = np.clip(c.real,0.0,None)
    return c
```
`np.fft.irfft` real by default.
Pressure:
```
def pressure_fft(a,p):
    n=len(a); m=len(p) #2n-1
    N=1 << (n + m - 2).bit_length()
    af=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    fp=np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    h=np.fft.irfft(af*fp, N)[:n+m-2]
    c = h[n-1:2*n-1]
    return np.clip(c.real,0.0,None)
```
Need p normalized sum1.

Mirror function details:
```
def mirror_run(a, n, rng, max_time, focus_start=1.0, focus_end=20.0, eta_start=0.2):
    a=np.array(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); a=a/a.sum()
    best=a.copy(); best_score=score_internal(a)
    focus=focus_start; eta=eta_start; stall=0
    for it in range(100000):
        if time>deadline: break
        q=conv_fft(a); M=q.max(); sc=2*n*M
        if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; stall=0
            eta=min(eta*1.05, 1.0)
        else:
            stall += 1
        # If stuck, perturb or adjust focus
        if stall > 1000:
            # multiplicative random perturbation
            a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0, 0.2, n)); a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=a.sum()
            stall=0
            eta=max(0.05, eta*0.5)
            focus=min(focus*1.5, focus_end)
        # compute p
        if M <=0: break
        T = M / focus  # temperature
        z = (q - M)/T  # <=0, max0
        p = np.exp(z); p /= p.sum()
        c = pressure_fft(a,p)
        c = np.maximum(c,0)
        mean=c.mean()
        if mean<=0: c = np.ones(n)/n
        else: c /= mean
        # damping log change
        delta = -eta*(c - 1.0)
        # Clip to avoid zeros
        delta = np.clip(delta, -3.0, 3.0)
        a_new = a * np.exp(delta)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12)
        a_new /= a_new.sum()
        # optional: if score(new) worse by factor, revert? We can check every iter cheap.
        sc_new = internal_score(a_new) # requires conv; we already computed q but not new. Could skip; but to avoid divergence, periodic.
        a=a_new
        # increase focus slowly
        if it % 200 ==0: focus = min(focus*1.02, focus_end)
    return best,best_score
```
This will compute conv every iteration plus for score new maybe extra. We can avoid sc_new each iter; just trust. But if update bad, stall increments and eventually perturb.

We can accelerate by computing q once; score =2nM. No extra internal_score.

Top-level:
```
def softmax_objective(x, beta):
    a=softmax(x)
    q=conv_fft(a)
    z=beta*q
    zm=z.max()
    return float(zm + np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))
```
Jacobian as above.
But `conv_fft` uses `len(a)` from x length. Good.
`weights` p = exp(z-zm)/sum; pressure.
Potential if beta huge and q small, z stable due subtract max; okay.

We can also use `scipy.optimize.fmin` (Nelder-Mead) on a reduced block-parameterization. This may find global in low-dim. Let's implement a "piecewise continuous optimizer" with K blocks and then refine full sequence.

Block parameterization:
- Sample K (e.g., 2 to 12).
- Generate random breakpoints (proportions) sorted, lengths l_i.
- Generate random positive heights h_i.
- Create sequence of length n by assigning each index to block based on breakpoints (e.g., `a = np.repeat(h, lengths)` with last adjust). Use n maybe 128 or 256.
- Run mirror/L-BFGS from this.
This is basically random step starts.

We can create starts:
```
def asymmetric_templates(n, rng):
  a=[]
  # template 0: random positive
  a.append(rng.gamma(1,n))
  # template 1: random step: K=3..10 random heights, lengths
  for K...:
    breaks=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); edges=[0]+breaks+[1]; lens=round...
    heights=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,K))
    a=np.repeat(heights,lens)[:n]
  # template 2: sum of Gaussians:
    centers=sorted(random); amps=random positive; widths=random; sum exp(-((t-c)/w)^2)
  # template 3: soft sigmoid steps: cumulative random coefficients
  # template 4: polynomial basis positive: vals = exp(∑ β_j t^j) with random β_j clipped nonnegative
  # template 5: known decreasing with oscillation: a=exp(-r t)*(1+A sin(2π f t+φ)) clipped.
```
Normalize, clip min.

We can run a time-sliced loop over starts and n.

Simpler:
```
def propose_candidate(seed=42,budget_s=1800):
   rng=default_rng(seed)
   deadline=now+budget_s-10
   best_seq = fallback n4
   best_score = evaluate_sequence(fallback)
   lengths=[16,32,64,128,256,512]
   # Use 70% for mirror, 20% polish, 10% local
   t_mirror_end = now + 0.7*(budget-10)
   for n in lengths:
      if now > t_mirror_end: break
      starts = max(5, int(2000 / sqrt(n))) maybe.
      for s in range(starts):
         if now > t_mirror_end: break
         a=make_start(n,rng,s)
         # allocate time_slice = min(remaining*0.1, 12) for small n, 5 for large
         a, sc = mirror_run(a,n,rng,time_slice=...)
         if sc < best_score: update
   # Polish best with L-BFGS for its n (if n<=256) and maybe one or two other bests
   for candidate in best_candidates:
      if now > t_polish_end: break
      cand = lbfgs_polish(cand, n, time_slice)
      update
   # final local perturbation
   if now < deadline: local_perturb(best)
   # final exact evaluate
   final=evaluate_sequence(best.tolist()); if final<best_score: best_score=final
   return best.tolist()
```

def basis_sequence(theta, n):
    t=(np.arange(n)/max(1,n-1))
    feats = [t**0, t, t*t, t**3, sin(2πt), cos(2πt), sin(4πt), cos(4πt), sin(6πt), cos(6πt), maybe |t-0.3|...]
    X=...
    z = X @ theta
    z -= z.max()
    a=np.exp(z)
    return a/a.sum()
```

Top-level functions:
```
def make_features(n): ... return X (n,D)
def seq_from_theta(theta, X):
    z=X@theta; z-=z.max(); e=np.exp(z); a=e/e.sum(); return a
def basis_objective(theta, beta, X):
    a=seq_from_theta(theta,X); q=conv_fft(a); z=beta*q; zm=z.max(); return zm+np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm)))
def basis_jacobian(theta, beta, X):
    a=seq...
    q=...; p=...
    Ga=2.0*beta*pressure
    Ma = X.T @ a  # D
    # grad_j = a.Ga? Actually sum_i Ga_i * a_i*(X_ij - Ma_j)
    # Let V = a*Ga (elementwise)
    # grad = X.T @ V - Ma * dot(V)
    V = a * Ga
    grad = X.T @ V - Ma * float(np.dot(V,1))
    return grad
```
Sign? ∂a/∂θ_j = a_i (X_ij - Ma_j). Gradient θ_j = ∑ Ga_i a_i (X_ij - Ma_j) = ∑ V_i X_ij - Ma_j ∑ V_i. Correct. Since z shift invariant? Features include constant; gradient may not sum zero in θ direction but fine.

def polish_basis(theta, n, deadline, beta_target=5.0, max_iter_stage=200, stages=6):
   X=make_features(n)
   for stage in range(stages):
      if time>deadline: break
      a=seq; q=conv; M=q.max(); beta=beta_target/M
      res=optimize(basis_objective, theta, jac=basis_jacobian, args=(beta,X,1e-4), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':max_iter_stage, 'ftol':1e-12})
      theta=res.x
      a=seq; score internal; update best
      beta_target = min(beta_target*1.5, 30.0)
   return best a/score
```

def random_start_template(n, ttype, rng):
  if ttype==0: a=rng.gamma(1.0,n)
  elif ttype==1: # lognormal trend
      t=np.linspace(0,1,n); a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1)*t*rng.uniform(0,3)); a*=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.5))
  elif ttype==2: # sum gaussians
      K=1+rng.integers(0,4); centers=sorted(rng.uniform(0,1,K)); widths=rng.uniform(0.05,0.35,K); amps=rng.uniform(0.2,1,K); a=sum(amps*gust)
  elif ttype==3: # random step with K
      K=3+rng.integers(0,8); edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1)); lens... heights=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,K))
  elif ttype==4: # sine mix clip
      t; a=1+sum amp*sin(freq*(2πt)+phase); a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
  else: # basis theta
      theta=rng.normal(0,1,make_features(n).shape[1]); a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
  a=np.maximum(a,0); a/=a.sum(); return a
```

```
def make_features(n):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cols=[]
    cols.append(np.ones(n))
    for p in range(1,5): cols.append(t**p)
    for f in (1,2,3):
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t))
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t))
    cols.append(np.exp(-0.5*((t-0.3)/0.15)**2))
    return np.column_stack(cols) # 1+4+6+1=12
```

def internal_score(arr):
    a=np.asarray(arr,float)
    a=np.maximum(a,0)
    s=a.sum()

    q=conv_fft(a/s) # score scale invariant; using normalized reduces beta? Internal score independent.
    return float(2*a.size*max(q.max(),0)/1.0)
```

def full_lbfgs_polish(a, n, deadline, max_stages=6, stage_maxiter=100):
   x=np.log(a+1e-12)
   x = x - x.mean()
   best=a; best_score=internal_score_norm(a)
   target=4.0
   for stage in range(max_stages):
      if time>deadline: break
      q=conv_fft(softmax_from_x(x)); M=q.max(); beta=target/M
      res=optimize(softmax_objective, x, jac=softmax_jacobian, args=(beta,), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':stage_maxiter, 'ftol':1e-12, 'maxfun':300})
      if res.x finite: x=res.x; x=x-x.mean()
      a=softmax_from_x(x)
      sc=...
      update best
      target=min(target*1.7, 40.0)
   return best,best_score
```

```
def upsample_sequence(a, n):
   m=len(a)
   x_old=np.linspace(0,1,m)
   x_new=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   b=np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
   b=np.maximum(b,1e-12)
   return b/b.sum()
```

Let's write `mirror_run` with a `max_iters` cap e.g., 200000.
```
def mirror_run(a, n, rng, time_limit):
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_deadline = start + max(0.1, float(time_limit))
    a=np.asarray(a,float).copy(); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 or not finite: a=np.ones(n)/n else a/=s
    best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score_norm(a)
    focus=3.0; focus_max=30.0; eta=0.25; stall=0; it=0
    while time.monotonic() < hard_deadline:
       it+=1
       q=conv_fft(a); M=float(q.max()); sc=2.0*n*M
       if sc < best_score - 1e-12:
           best=a.copy(); best_score=sc; stall=0
           eta=min(1.0, eta*1.03)
       else:
           stall += 1
           if stall > 2000:
               # restore best and perturb
               a=best.copy()
               a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, n))
               a=np.maximum(a,1e-12); a/=a.sum()
               stall=0; eta=max(0.05, eta*0.6); focus=min(focus_max, focus*1.5)
           elif stall % 500 ==0:
               eta=max(0.05, eta*0.85); focus=min(focus_max, focus*1.2)
       if M <= 0: break
       T = M / focus
       z = (q - M)/T
       # due floating z max 0, min maybe -large
       p = np.exp(z)
       psum = p.sum()
       if not finite or psum<=0: p=np.ones(2*n-1)/(2*n-1)
       else: p/=psum
       c=pressure_fft(a,p)
       c=np.maximum(c,0)
       cmean=float(c.mean())
       if cmean<=0 or not finite:
           c=np.ones(n)/n
       else:
           c/=cmean
       delta = -eta*(c - 1.0)
       delta = np.clip(delta, -3.0, 3.0)
       a_new = a * np.exp(delta)
       a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12)
       snew=float(a_new.sum())
       if not finite or snew<=0:
           a_new=a
       else:
           a_new/=snew
           # optional: if score deteriorated too much, revert? We can compute qnew every 100? let's compute every 50 to avoid bad drift
           if it % 50 == 0:
               qnew=conv_fft(a_new); Mnew=float(qnew.max()); scnew=2.0*n*Mnew
               if scnew > best_score*1.05 + 1e-12:
                   # maybe revert to previous a? We have a before; do a=a_new? Keep to explore? Revert to be safe.
                   a=a
                   stall += 100
               else:
                   a=a_new
           else:
               a=a_new
       if it % 100 == 0:
           focus=min(focus_max, focus*1.01)
       if it > 200000: break
    # ensure best updated if a better at end
    q=conv_fft(a); M=q.max(); sc=2nM; if sc<best_score: best=a
    return best, best_score
```
The check every 50 with `best_score*1.05` may revert even if a_new not best but not too bad? If current a had score much worse than best (after perturb), best_score*1.05 may be lower than a_new, causing revert repeatedly to bad a? Actually if a is currently bad (after perturb) and best is good, best_score small. Perturb sets a=best, so not bad. If a drifts bad, revert to previous a. Good.

But `a` variable in loop overwritten; if check reverts to previous `a`, we need preserve previous. In code, `a` before update is previous. In check, if reject, `a = previous_a` (but variable previous updated? We'll store `a_old = a` before update). Simpler:
```
a_old=a
...
if reject: a=a_old else a=a_new
```
Good.

`random_start` function:
```
def make_random_start(n, templ, rng):
    t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
    if templ % 7 ==0:
       a=rng.gamma(1.0,n)
    elif templ %7 ==1:
       a=np.exp(rng.normal(0,0.8) * (rng.uniform(0,2)*t - rng.uniform(0,1)))
       a += 1e-3
    elif templ%7==2:
       a=np.exp(-rng.uniform(0.5,3)*t) * (1+0.5*np.sin(2π*t*rng.uniform(1,3)+...))
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-4)
    elif templ%7==3:
       K=int(rng.integers(2,6))
       centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K))
       widths=rng.uniform(0.05,0.3,K)
       amps=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0,K)
       a=np.zeros(n)
       for c,w,amp in zip: a += amp*np.exp(-0.5*((t-c)/w)**2)
       a +=0.01
    elif templ%7==4:
       K=int(rng.integers(3,10))
       edges=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,K-1))
       # lens
       breaks=np.concatenate(([0],edges,[1]))
       idx=np.digitize(t, breaks[1:-1], right=False)
       heights=np.exp(rng.normal(0,1,K))
       a=np.repeat(heights, n)?? Need map to n.
       # assign by proportions:
       a=np.empty(n); for i in range(n): j=int(t[i]*K); a[i]=heights[min(j,K-1)] + small rng
       a += 0.01
    elif templ%7==5:
       # sine mix
       a=1.0
       for f in (1,2,3):
          a += rng.normal(0,0.5)*np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t + rng.uniform(0,2π))
       a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
    else:
       X=make_features(n); theta=rng.normal(0,1,X.shape[1]); a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
    a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); a/=a.sum(); return a
```
No lambda, loops okay.

`upsample_sequence` as above.

Let's do in basis search: For some starts, use random features:
```
def make_random_features(n, D, rng):
   t=np.linspace(0,1,n)
   cols=[np.ones(n)]
   for _ in range(D-1):
       freq=float(rng.uniform(0.5,6.0)); phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2π))
       if rng.random()<0.5: col=np.sin(2π*freq*t+phase)
       else: col=np.cos(2π*freq*t+phase)
       cols.append(col)
   # add a random linear trend
   cols[1]=t
   return np.column_stack(cols)
```
Then optimize with that X. This can find asymmetric bump/oscillatory shapes. D=12. Random features may be redundant but okay. We can use fixed features for half starts, random for half. This increases chance.

Let's detail `basis_objective`:
```
def basis_objective(theta, beta, X, lam):
    a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
    if a is None: return 1e100
    q=conv_fft(a)
    M=float(q.max())
    if M<=0: val=0.0
    else:
        z=beta*q
        zm=float(z.max())
        val=zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z-zm))))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val=1e100
    if lam !=0:
        val += lam*float(np.dot(theta,theta))
    return float(val)
```
This beta fixed; if q max changes, beta maybe from initial target/M0. If M0 small, beta large. Good.

`basis_jacobian` similar, returns gradient of same objective. Need if objective nonfinite return zeros? For stability:
```
if bad: return np.zeros(D) + 2*lam*theta
```
But optimizer may stall; okay.

`softmax_objective(x,beta,lam)`:
```
a=softmax_from_x(x); q=conv_fft(a); z=beta*q; val=...
val += lam*dot(x,x)
```
`softmax_jacobian`: if bad return 2lam*x. `S=float(np.dot(Ga,a)); grad=a*(S-Ga); grad += 2lam*x`.

`seq_from_theta` returns None if not finite. But objective wants scalar. We'll handle:
```
def seq_from_theta(theta,X):
   z=X@theta
   z-=np.max(z)
   e=np.exp(z)
   s=e.sum()
   if not finite or s<=0: return None
   return e/s
```

`softmax_from_x` similarly.

`conv_fft`: for a not normalized? It can take any; if n=0? no.
```
def conv_fft(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   n=a.size
   if n==0: return np.array([0.0])
   N=next_pow2(2*n-1)
   fa=np.fft.rfft(a,N)
   c=np.fft.irfft(fa*fa,N)
   c=c[:2*n-1]
   c=np.real(c)
   return np.clip(c,0.0,None)
```
If N=1, irfft length 1. Good.

`pressure_fft`:
```
def pressure_fft(a,p):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); p=np.asarray(p,float)
   n=a.size; m=p.size
   L=n+m-2
   if L<=0: return np.ones(n)/n
   N=next_pow2(L)
   af=np.fft.rfft(a[::-1],N)
   fp=np.fft.rfft(p,N)
   h=np.fft.irfft(af*fp,N)[:L]
   c=np.real(h[n-1:n-1+n])
   return np.clip(c,0.0,None)
```
If N maybe less than m? next_pow2 L ensures enough. `irfft` with n=N returns length N, okay.

`internal_score`:
```
def internal_score(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float)
   if a.size==0: return inf
   a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
   s=float(a.sum())
   if not finite or s<1e-12: return inf
   q=conv_fft(a/s)
   M=float(q.max())
   if not finite: return inf
   return float(2*a.size*M)
```
`internal_score_norm` assumes sum1 but can normalize:
```
def internal_score_norm(a):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,0); s=a.sum(); if s<1e-15 return inf; a=a/s; q=conv_fft(a); return 2*len(a)*q.max()
```

`make_features(n)`:
```
def make_features(n):
    t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
    cols=[np.ones(n)]
    for p in range(1,5): cols.append(t**p)
    for f in (1,2,3):
       cols.append(np.sin(2*np.pi*f*t))
       cols.append(np.cos(2*np.pi*f*t))
    cols.append(np.exp(-0.5*((t-0.3)/0.15)**2))
    cols.append(np.exp(-0.5*((t-0.7)/0.15)**2))
    return np.column_stack(cols)
```

```
def make_random_start(n, mode, rng):
   t=np.linspace(0.0,1.0,n)
   m = mode % 7
   if m==0: a=rng.gamma(1.0,size=n)
   elif m==1:
      slope=float(rng.uniform(-2.0,2.0)); intercept=float(rng.uniform(-1.0,1.0))
      a=np.exp(slope*t + intercept + rng.normal(0,0.3,size=n))
   elif m==2:
      drift=float(rng.uniform(0.0,2.0)); freq=float(rng.uniform(0.5,4.0)); phase=float(rng.uniform(0,2*np.pi))
      a=np.exp(-drift*t) * np.maximum(0.05, 1.0 + float(rng.uniform(0.2,0.8))*np.sin(2*np.pi*freq*t+phase))
   elif m==3:
      K=int(rng.integers(2,6)); centers=np.sort(rng.uniform(0,1,size=K)); widths=np.exp(rng.normal(-2.0,0.5,size=K)); amps=rng.uniform(0.3,1.0,size=K)
      a=np.zeros(n)
      for idx in range(K):
         a += amps[idx]*np.exp(-0.5*((t-centers[idx])/widths[idx])**2)
      a += 0.05
   elif m==4:
      K=int(rng.integers(3,10)); heights=np.exp(rng.normal(0.0,0.8,size=K))
      a=np.zeros(n)
      for i in range(n):
         j=int(t[i]*K); if j>=K: j=K-1; a[i]=heights[j]
      a += float(np.max(heights))*0.01
   elif m==5:
      a=1.0 + 0.4*np.sin(...)
      for f in (1,2,3): a += rng.normal(0,0.4)*...
      a=np.maximum(a,1e-3)
   else:
      X=make_features(n); theta=rng.normal(0,1,size=X.shape[1]); a=seq_from_theta(theta,X); if a is None: a=np.ones(n)
   a=np.asarray(a,float); a=np.maximum(a,1e-6); s=float(a.sum()); if bad: a=np.ones(n)/n; else a/=s; return a
```
Mode can be integer increment.

`basis_lbfgs_run`: Need maybe if `basis_objective` initial nonfinite. We'll set options with `maxiter`, `ftol`. Use `timeout`? scipy minimize doesn't have timeout. But maxiter bounded.
```
def basis_lbfgs_run(theta, n, X, deadline, stage_maxiter=40, stages=6, target=4.0):
   theta=np.asarray(theta,float).copy()
   best_a=None; best_score=inf
   for stage in range(stages):
       if time.monotonic() > deadline: break
       a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
       if a is None:
          theta = np.zeros(X.shape[1]) * 0.1
          a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
          continue
       q=conv_fft(a); M=float(q.max())
       if M<=0 or not finite: beta=1.0
       else: beta=target/M
       try:
          res=minimize(basis_objective, theta, jac=basis_jacobian, args=(beta,X,1e-6), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter': stage_maxiter, 'ftol': 1e-12, 'maxfun': stage_maxiter*2})
       except Exception:
          break
       if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
          theta=res.x
       a=seq_from_theta(theta,X)
       if a is None: break
       sc=2.0*n*float(conv_fft(a).max())  # compute q? We can use q from last? recompute.
       if sc < best_score:
          best=a.copy(); best_score=sc
       target = min(40.0, target*1.7)
   if best is None: best=np.ones(n)/n; best_score=inf
   return best, best_score
```
Recomputing conv for score; could reuse q from before optimize? Not with new theta. Fine.

def full_lbfgs_polish(a, n, deadline, stage_maxiter=60, stages=5, target=4.0):
   a=np.asarray(a,float); ...
   x=np.log(a+1e-12)-np.log(a+1e-12).mean()
   best=a.copy(); best_score=internal_score_norm(a)
   for stage ...:
      a=softmax_from_x(x); q=conv_fft(a); M=q.max(); beta=target/M if M>0 else1
      res=minimize(softmax_objective, x, jac=softmax_jacobian, args=(beta,1e-6), method='L-BFGS-B', options={'maxiter':stage_maxiter,'ftol':1e-12})
      x=res.x - mean
      a=softmax_from_x(x); sc=...
      update
      target=min(40,target*1.6)
   return best,best_score
```

`add_candidate` implementation with sorted insertion. It will keep sorted by score. Let's write:
```
def add_candidate(cands, a, score, cap=18):
    if a is None or score is None: return
    a=np.asarray(a,float)
    if a.size==0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)): return
    a=np.clip(a,0,1000)
    if float(a.sum())<0.01: return
    a=a/a.sum() # for storage? If we normalize, internal score remains same. Good.
    score=float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score): return
    inserted=False
    for i in range(len(cands)):
       if score < cands[i][0]:
          cands.insert(i,(score,a.copy()))
          inserted=True
          break
    if not inserted and len(cands)<cap:
       cands.append((score,a.copy()))
    # if full and score >= existing first? Since sorted, if no insert before existing and len>=cap, do nothing. If len<insert appended.
    while len(cands)>cap:
       cands.pop()
```
If len full and score larger than worst, inserted false and len not <cap, nothing. Good. If len full and score smaller than some, insertion before i, len=cap+1 then pop worst. Good.

Now, test failure modes:
- `basis_lbfgs_run` could take too long if `minimize` maxfun not limiting? `maxiter` limits iterations; each L-BFGS iteration may do several line search function evals, but maxfun option can limit. Set `maxfun = stage_maxiter * 3` to bound. Good.
- In `basis_objective`, if beta very large and z values large, exp(z-zm) okay. But zm maybe inf if beta*q has inf due beta large and q huge? q normalized sum1, max <=1 (if one element 1). beta=target/M; M maybe very small? If M=1e-12, beta huge 1e16, z huge but q*beta for max M gives target, for others q/M*target maybe ≤target; no overflow because (q-M) may be negative? We compute z=beta*q directly; beta*M=target, okay finite; beta*min maybe. If M very small but n large, q values small; beta/q? beta=target/M. If M=0? handle. If M underflows? M at least maybe 1e-300? Not with normalized nonnegative sum1; if n huge one element ~1e-6? M~1e-12. beta~1e16, beta*min q maybe small, but computing beta*q can overflow if q max? beta*M=target no. If M extremely small (1e-300), beta huge 1e301 overflow. But n not huge and sum1; M cannot be <1/n? It can be <1/n target/n ~1e-7 for n1000; not 1e-300. Safe.

Piecewise constant search idea:
- For each n (e.g., 96,144,192) and K (3..8), random split lengths and heights; optimize heights with L-BFGS on log heights (K vars) with sum constraint using softmax of height logits. This is even lower dimension and can exactly exploit block structure. But basis already can approximate blocks? Not exactly with smooth features. Step functions might be optimal? Perhaps optimal continuous is piecewise constant (many blocks). Adding this could help.

Implementing piecewise constant optimizer:
Parameters θ length K. For fixed block lengths l_j, sequence a: first l_1 entries = softmax(θ)_1, etc. Actually heights h=softmax(θ), then assign a_i=h[j], scale? If repeat h[j] for block j, sum = ∑ h[j]*l_j. To sum1, set a_i = h[j] / S where S=∑ h[j] l_j. But h already sum1 not important. We can use exp(θ) raw heights, then normalize entire sequence. Equivalent to weighted softmax: log contribution of block j = θ_j, but each height repeated l_j, total sequence sum S=∑ l_j e^{θ_j}. Logit for parameter θ: gradient wrt θ_j from Ga? If a_i = e^{θ_j}/S. ∂a_i/∂θ_k = a_i (δ_jk - l_k e^{θ_k}/S) = a_i(δ_jk - H_k) where H_k = S_k/S (block total mass). Gradient θ_k =∑_i Ga_i a_i(δ_jk-H_k) = ∑_{i in block k} Ga_i a_i - H_k ∑_i Ga_i a_i. H_k is block mass. Can compute from a: H_k = a[first of block]*l_k (if constant). This is nice. For block-structured optimization, dimension K small; can run L-BFGS on θ for random splits. This explores exact step patterns and may find good pieces. Let's add? It increases code but manageable.

Top-level:
```
def block_sequence(theta, lengths):
   # lengths list or array of ints sum n
   # a_i = exp(theta[block]) / sum(block_lengths * exp(theta))
   e=np.exp(theta-theta.max())
   S=np.dot(lengths, e)
   if S<=0 fail
   # need assign: could use np.repeat(e, lengths)/S
   a=np.repeat(e/ S, lengths) # length n
   return a
```

```python
import numpy as np
import time
from scipy.optimize import minimize


def next_pow2(m):
    if m <= 1:
        return 1
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def conv_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    if n == 0:
        return np.array([0.0])
    N = next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    fa = np.fft.rfft(a, N)
    c = np.fft.irfft(fa * fa, N)
    c = np.real(c[:2 * n - 1])
    return np.clip(c, 0.0, None)


def pressure_fft(a, p):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = a.size
    m = p.size
    L = n + m - 2
    if L <= 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / max(1, n)
    N = next_pow2(L)
    af = np.fft.rfft(a[::-1], N)
    fp = np.fft.rfft(p, N)
    h = np.fft.irfft(af * fp, N)
    c = np.real(h[:L][n - 1:n - 1 + n])
    return np.clip(c, 0.0, None)


def internal_score(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    q = conv_fft(a / s)
    m = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def internal_score_norm(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0 or not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return float("inf")
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 1e-15:
        return float("inf")
    a = a / s
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return float("inf")
    return float(2.0 * a.size * m)


def fallback_sequence():
    a = np.array([0.36, 0.26, 0.22, 0.16], dtype=np.float64)
    a = a / a.sum()
    return a


def add_candidate(cands, a, score, cap=18):
    if a is None or score is None:
        return
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
        return
    a = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s < 0.01:
        return
    a = a / s
    score = float(score)
    if not np.isfinite(score):
        return

    inserted = False
    for i in range(len(cands)):
        if score < cands[i][0]:
            cands.insert(i, (score, a.copy()))
            inserted = True
            break
    if not inserted and len(cands) < cap:
        cands.append((score, a.copy()))
    while len(cands) > cap:
        cands.pop()


def upsample_sequence(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    m = a.size
    if m == 0:
        return np.ones(n) / max(1, n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, m)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    b = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    b = np.maximum(b, 1e-12)
    s = float(b.sum())
    if s <= 0:
        return np.ones(n) / n
    return b / s


def seq_from_theta(theta, X):
    z = X @ theta
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return None
    return e / s


def make_features(n):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    for p in range(1, 5):
        cols.append(t ** p)
    for f in (1, 2, 3):
        cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t))
        cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t))
    cols.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - 0.3) / 0.15) ** 2))
    cols.append(np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - 0.7) / 0.15) ** 2))
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def make_random_features(n, D, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    D = max(2, int(D))
    cols = [np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)]
    cols.append(t.copy())
    for _ in range(1, D - 1):
        f = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 6.0))
        ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        if rng.random() < 0.5:
            cols.append(np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph))
        else:
            cols.append(np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph))
    c = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
    w = float(rng.uniform(0.10, 0.30))
    cols[-1] = np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - c) / w) ** 2)
    return np.column_stack(cols)


def basis_objective(theta, beta, X, lam):
    a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
    if a is None:
        return 1e100
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        val = 0.0
    else:
        z = beta * q
        zm = float(z.max())
        val = zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zm))))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    if lam != 0.0:
        val += lam * float(np.dot(theta, theta))
    return float(val)


def basis_jacobian(theta, beta, X, lam):
    D = len(theta)
    a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
    if a is None:
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    z = beta * q
    zm = float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    ps = float(w.sum())
    if ps <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        p = np.ones(q.size) / q.size
    else:
        p = w / ps
    c = pressure_fft(a, p)
    Ga = 2.0 * beta * c
    V = a * Ga
    Ma = X.T @ a
    sv = float(V.sum())
    grad = X.T @ V - Ma * sv + 2.0 * lam * theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    return grad


def basis_lbfgs_run(theta, n, X, deadline, stage_maxiter=50, stages=6, target=4.0):
    theta = np.asarray(theta, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    D = X.shape[1]
    if theta.size != D:
        theta = np.zeros(D, dtype=np.float64)
    best_a = np.ones(n) / n
    best_sc = internal_score(best_a)

    for _ in range(stages):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
        if a is None:
            theta = 0.1 * rng_noise[D:]  # not used
            a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
            if a is None:
                break
        q = conv_fft(a)
        m = float(q.max())
        if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
            beta = 1.0
        else:
            beta = float(target) / m
        try:
            res = minimize(
                basis_objective,
                theta,
                jac=basis_jacobian,
                args=(beta, X, 1e-6),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": int(stage_maxiter), "maxfun": int(stage_maxiter * 3), "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            theta = res.x
        a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
        if a is None:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(conv_fft(a).max())
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        target = min(40.0, target * 1.7)

    return best_a, best_sc


def softmax_from_x(x):
    z = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    z = z - float(z.max())
    e = np.exp(z)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        return None
    return e / s


def softmax_objective(x, beta, lam):
    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    if a is None:
        return 1e100
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        val = 0.0
    else:
        z = beta * q
        zm = float(z.max())
        val = zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zm))))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    if lam != 0.0:
        val += lam * float(np.dot(x, x))
    return float(val)


def softmax_jacobian(x, beta, lam):
    a = softmax_from_x(x)
    if a is None:
        return 2.0 * lam * x
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return 2.0 * lam * x
    z = beta * q
    zm = float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    ps = float(w.sum())
    if ps <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        p = np.ones(q.size) / q.size
    else:
        p = w / ps
    c = pressure_fft(a, p)
    Ga = 2.0 * beta * c
    V = a * Ga
    S = float(np.dot(a, Ga))
    grad = V - a * S + 2.0 * lam * x
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 2.0 * lam * x
    return grad


def full_lbfgs_polish(a, n, deadline, stage_maxiter=50, stages=5, target=4.0):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if s <= 0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        a = np.ones(n) / n
    else:
        a = a / s
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_norm(a)
    x = np.log(a + 1e-12)
    x = x - float(x.mean())

    for _ in range(stages):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        cur = softmax_from_x(x)
        if cur is None:
            break
        q = conv_fft(cur)
        m = float(q.max())
        if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
            beta = 1.0
        else:
            beta = float(target) / m
        try:
            res = minimize(
                softmax_objective,
                x,
                jac=softmax_jacobian,
                args=(beta, 1e-6),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": int(stage_maxiter), "maxfun": int(stage_maxiter * 3), "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            x = res.x
        x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
        x = x - float(x.mean())
        cur = softmax_from_x(x)
        if cur is None:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(conv_fft(cur).max())
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = cur.copy()
        target = min(40.0, target * 1.6)

    return best_a, best_sc


def make_random_start(n, mode, rng):
    t = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    m = int(mode) % 7
    if m == 0:
        a = rng.gamma(1.0, size=n)
    elif m == 1:
        slope = float(rng.uniform(-2.0, 2.0))
        intercept = float(rng.uniform(-1.0, 1.0))
        a = np.exp(slope * t + intercept + rng.normal(0.0, 0.3, size=n))
    elif m == 2:
        drift = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0))
        freq = float(rng.uniform(0.5, 4.0))
        phase = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        amp = float(rng.uniform(0.2, 0.8))
        a = np.exp(-drift * t) * np.maximum(0.05, 1.0 + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * freq * t + phase))
    elif m == 3:
        K = int(rng.integers(2, 6))
        centers = np.sort(rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0, size=K))
        widths = np.exp(rng.normal(-2.0, 0.5, size=K))
        amps = rng.uniform(0.3, 1.0, size=K)
        a = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for idx in range(K):
            a += amps[idx] * np.exp(-0.5 * ((t - centers[idx]) / widths[idx]) ** 2)
        a += 0.05
    elif m == 4:
        K = int(rng.integers(3, 10))
        heights = np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.8, size=K))
        idx = np.minimum(np.floor(t * K).astype(int), K - 1)
        a = heights[idx]
        a += 0.01 * float(heights.max())
    elif m == 5:
        a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        for f in (1, 2, 3):
            ph = float(rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
            amp = float(rng.normal(0.0, 0.45))
            a += amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * f * t + ph)
        a = np.maximum(a, 1e-3)
    else:
        X = make_features(n)
        theta = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=X.shape[1])
        a = seq_from_theta(theta, X)
        if a is None:
            a = np.ones(n)
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.maximum(a, 1e-6)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0:
        a = np.ones(n)
        s = float(n)
    return a / s


def mirror_run(a, n, rng, time_limit):
    start = time.monotonic()
    hard_dl = start + max(0.25, float(time_limit))
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0 or a.size == 0:
        a = np.ones(max(1, n)) / max(1, n)
    else:
        a = a / s
    n = a.size
    best_a = a.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_norm(a)
    focus = 3.0
    focus_max = 35.0
    eta = 0.25
    stall = 0
    it = 0

    while time.monotonic() < hard_dl:
        it += 1
        q = conv_fft(a)
        m = float(q.max())
        sc = 2.0 * n * m
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
            stall = 0
            eta = min(1.0, eta * 1.03)
        else:
            stall += 1
            if stall > 2500:
                a = best_a.copy()
                a *= np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, 0.25, size=n))
                a = np.maximum(a, 1e-12)
                a /= a.sum()
                stall = 0
                eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.6)
                focus = min(focus_max, focus * 1.5)
            elif stall % 500 == 0:
                eta = max(0.05, eta * 0.85)
                focus = min(focus_max, focus * 1.2)

        if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
            break

        temp = m / focus
        z = (q - m) / temp
        p = np.exp(z)
        ps = float(p.sum())
        if ps <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
            p = np.ones(q.size) / q.size
        else:
            p = p / ps

        c = pressure_fft(a, p)
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        cm = float(c.mean())
        if cm <= 0 or not np.isfinite(cm):
            c = np.ones(n) / n
        else:
            c = c / cm

        delta = -eta * (c - 1.0)
        delta = np.clip(delta, -3.0, 3.0)
        a_new = a * np.exp(delta)
        a_new = np.maximum(a_new, 1e-12)
        sn = float(a_new.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(sn) or sn <= 0:
            a_new = a
        else:
            a_new = a_new / sn
            a_old = a
            accepted = True
            if it % 50 == 0:
                qn = conv_fft(a_new)
                mn = float(qn.max())
                scn = 2.0 * n * mn
                if np.isfinite(scn) and scn > best_sc * 1.05 + 1e-10:
                    accepted = False
            if accepted:
                a = a_new
            else:
                a = a_old

        if it % 100 == 0:
            focus = min(focus_max, focus * 1.01)
        if it > 200000:
            break

    q = conv_fft(a)
    sc = 2.0 * n * float(q.max())
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
        best_sc = sc
        best_a = a.copy()
    return best_a, best_sc


def make_block_params(n, K, rng):
    K = max(1, min(int(K), int(n)))
    if K == 1:
        lengths = np.array([n], dtype=np.int64)
        block_ids = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.int64)
        return lengths, block_ids
    d = rng.gamma(1.0, size=K)
    lengths = (n * d / d.sum()).astype(np.int64)
    lengths = np.maximum(lengths, 1)
    while int(lengths.sum()) > n:
        idx = int(np.argmax(lengths))
        if lengths[idx] > 1:
            lengths[idx] -= 1
        else:
            break
    while int(lengths.sum()) < n:
        idx = int(rng.integers(0, K))
        lengths[idx] += 1
    block_ids = np.repeat(np.arange(K, dtype=np.int64), lengths)
    return lengths, block_ids


def block_sequence(theta, lengths):
    e = np.exp(theta - float(theta.max()))
    S = float(np.dot(lengths, e))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0:
        return None
    h = e / S
    return np.repeat(h, lengths)


def block_objective(theta, beta, block_ids, lengths, lam):
    a = block_sequence(theta, lengths)
    if a is None:
        return 1e100
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        val = 0.0
    else:
        z = beta * q
        zm = float(z.max())
        val = zm + float(np.log(np.sum(np.exp(z - zm))))
    if not np.isfinite(val):
        val = 1e100
    if lam != 0.0:
        val += lam * float(np.dot(theta, theta))
    return float(val)


def block_jacobian(theta, beta, block_ids, lengths, lam):
    e = np.exp(theta - float(theta.max()))
    S = float(np.dot(lengths, e))
    if not np.isfinite(S) or S <= 0:
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    h = e / S
    a = np.repeat(h, lengths)
    q = conv_fft(a)
    m = float(q.max())
    if m <= 0 or not np.isfinite(m):
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    z = beta * q
    zm = float(z.max())
    w = np.exp(z - zm)
    ps = float(w.sum())
    if ps <= 0 or not np.isfinite(ps):
        p = np.ones(q.size) / q.size
    else:
        p = w / ps
    c = pressure_fft(a, p)
    Ga = 2.0 * beta * c
    V = a * Ga
    K = len(theta)
    block_sums = np.bincount(block_ids, weights=V, minlength=K)
    H = lengths.astype(np.float64) * h
    sv = float(V.sum())
    grad = block_sums - H * sv + 2.0 * lam * theta
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(grad)):
        return 2.0 * lam * theta
    return grad


def block_lbfgs_run(lengths, block_ids, theta0, n, deadline, stage_maxiter=80, stages=5, target=4.0):
    theta = np.asarray(theta0, dtype=np.float64).copy()
    K = len(theta)
    if K != len(lengths):
        theta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    a0 = block_sequence(theta, lengths)
    if a0 is None:
        theta = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
        a0 = block_sequence(theta, lengths)
    if a0 is None:
        a0 = np.ones(n) / n
    best_a = a0.copy()
    best_sc = internal_score_norm(a0)

    for _ in range(stages):
        if time.monotonic() > deadline:
            break
        a = block_sequence(theta, lengths)
        if a is None:
            break
        q = conv_fft(a)
        m = float(q.max())
        if m <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(m):
            beta = 1.0
        else:
            beta = float(target) / m
        try:
            res = minimize(
                block_objective,
                theta,
                jac=block_jacobian,
                args=(beta, block_ids, lengths, 1e-6),
                method="L-BFGS-B",
                options={"maxiter": int(stage_maxiter), "maxfun": int(stage_maxiter * 3), "ftol": 1e-12},
            )
        except Exception:
            break
        if res.x is not None and np.all(np.isfinite(res.x)):
            theta = res.x
        a = block_sequence(theta, lengths)
        if a is None:
            break
        sc = 2.0 * n * float(conv_fft(a).max())
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best_a = a.copy()
        target = min(40.0, target * 1.7)

    return best_a, best_sc


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=1800, **kwargs):
    try:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed))
        start_time = time.monotonic()
        total = max(1.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
        deadline = start_time + total

        cands = []
        fb = fallback_sequence()
        add_candidate(cands, fb, internal_score(fb))

        try:
            par = np.asarray(height_sequence_1, dtype=np.float64)
            if par.ndim == 1 and par.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(par)):
                add_candidate(cands, par, internal_score(par))
        except Exception:
            par = None

        # Phase 1: low-dimensional smooth-basis search.
        t_basis = start_time + 0.32 * total
        basis_configs = [
            (64, 30, 70),
            (128, 45, 50),
            (256, 30, 35),
            (512, 15, 25),
        ]
        for n, num_starts, iters in basis_configs:
            if time.monotonic() > t_basis:
                break
            if n <= 0:
                continue
            Xfixed = make_features(n)
            D = Xfixed.shape[1]
            for s in range(num_starts):
                if time.monotonic() > t_basis:
                    break
                if s % 2 == 0:
                    X = Xfixed
                else:
                    X = make_random_features(n, D, rng)
                theta0 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=X.shape[1]) * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
                a, sc = basis_lbfgs_run(theta0, n, X, t_basis, stage_maxiter=iters)
                if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                    add_candidate(cands, a, sc)
                    if n <= 128:
                        ns = min(512, max(32, n * 2))
                        a2 = upsample_sequence(a, ns)
                        add_candidate(cands, a2, internal_score(a2))
                if t_basis - time.monotonic() < 3.0:
                    break

        # Phase 2: low-dimensional piecewise-constant search.
        t_block = start_time + 0.47 * total
        block_configs = [
            (64, 3, 12),
            (64, 4, 12),
            (128, 5, 12),
            (160, 6, 9),
            (224, 7, 7),
        ]
        for n, K, num_starts in block_configs:
            if time.monotonic() > t_block:
                break
            for s in range(num_starts):
                if time.monotonic() > t_block or t_block - time.monotonic() < 1.5:
                    break
                lengths, block_ids = make_block_params(n, K, rng)
                theta0 = rng.normal(0.0, 1.0, size=len(lengths)) * rng.uniform(0.5, 2.0)
                a, sc = block_lbfgs_run(lengths, block_ids, theta0, n, t_block, stage_maxiter=80)
                if a is not None and np.isfinite(sc):
                    add_candidate(cands, a, sc)
                    if n <= 128:
                        ns = min(512, max(32, n * 2))
                        a2 = upsample_sequence(a, ns)
                        add_candidate(cands, a2, internal_score(a2))

        print("after low-dim search", np.asarray([cands[0][0]]).item())

        # Phase 3: full mirror/multiplicative search on good starts and random starts.
        t_mirror = start_time + 0.82 * total

        mirrored = 0
        for sc, a in list(cands):
            if mirrored >= 10 or time.monotonic() > t_mirror:
                break
            n = len(a)
            if n > 1024:
                continue
            if n <= 64:
                sl = 2.5
            elif n <= 128:
                sl = 4.0
            elif n <= 256:
                sl = 5.5
            else:
                sl = 7.0
            rem = t_mirror - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 0.5:
                break
            sl = min(sl, rem * 0.9)
            a2, sc2 = mirror_run(a, n, rng, sl)
            if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2):
                add_candidate(cands, a2, sc2)
            mirrored += 1
            if t_mirror - time.monotonic() < 3.0:
                break

        mode = 0
        random_configs = [
            (128, 28, 1.2),
            (256, 20, 2.2),
            (512, 12, 3.2),
        ]
        for n, count, sl in random_configs:
            for _ in range(count):
                if time.monotonic() > t_mirror:
                    break
                rem = t_mirror - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 0.4:
                    break
                a = make_random_start(n, mode, rng)
                mode += 1
                a2, sc2 = mirror_run(a, n, rng, min(sl, rem * 0.9))
                if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2):
                    add_candidate(cands, a2, sc2)

        # Extra random mirror if still time before polish.
        if time.monotonic() < t_mirror - 2.0:
            mode = 50
            for _ in range(30):
                if time.monotonic() > t_mirror:
                    break
                rem = t_mirror - time.monotonic()
                if rem < 0.3:
                    break
                n = int(rng.choice([128, 256]))
                a = make_random_start(n, mode, rng)
                mode += 1
                a2, sc2 = mirror_run(a, n, rng, min(1.5, rem * 0.9))
                if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2):
                    add_candidate(cands, a2, sc2)

        print("after mirror search", np.asarray([cands[0][0]]).item())

        # Phase 4: polish top candidates with full-sequence L-BFGS.
        polished = 0
        for sc, a in list(cands):
            if polished >= 10 or time.monotonic() > deadline - 3.0:
                break
            n = len(a)
            if n > 768:
                continue
            rem = deadline - time.monotonic()
            if rem < 1.0:
                break
            td = time.monotonic() + min(4.5, rem * 0.9)
            a2, sc2 = full_lbfgs_polish(a, n, td)
            if a2 is not None and np.isfinite(sc2):
                add_candidate(cands, a2, sc2)
            polished += 1
            if deadline - time.monotonic() < 6.0:
                break

        # Final exact selection.
        chosen_a = None
        chosen_score = float("inf")
        for sc, a in cands[:30]:
            if not np.all(np.isfinite(a)):
                continue
            arr = np.clip(a, 0.0, 1000.0)
            if arr.size < 1 or arr.sum() < 0.01:
                continue
            if arr.size > 12000:
                continue
            try:
                exact = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                exact = float("inf")
            if np.isfinite(exact) and exact < chosen_score:
                chosen_score = exact
                chosen_a = arr.copy()

        if chosen_a is None:
            arr = np.clip(fb, 0.0, 1000.0)
            chosen_a = arr
            try:
                chosen_score = float(evaluate_sequence(arr.tolist()))
            except Exception:
                chosen_score = float("inf")

        if par is not None:
            try:
                pass_seq = np.clip(np.asarray(par, dtype=np.float64), 0.0, 1000.0)
                if pass_seq.size <= 12000 and pass_seq.sum() >= 0.01:
                    ps = float(evaluate_sequence(pass_seq.tolist()))
                    if np.isfinite(ps) and ps < chosen_score:
                        chosen_score = ps
                        chosen_a = pass_seq.copy()
            except Exception:
                pass

        assert chosen_a is not None
        return [float(x) for x in np.asarray(chosen_a, dtype=np.float64)]
    except Exception:
        return [0.36, 0.26, 0.22, 0.16]
```
